<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-42297C05-D0A4-48DE-A8F4-3791DDC7D537'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Leyla buys a bracelet costing $\pounds6.49$. If she pays with a $\pounds10$  note what change should she receive?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Change received}  &= 10 - 6.49 \\\\
                         &= \pounds 3.51
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 3.51$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Change received}  &= 10 - 6.49 \\\\
                         &= \pounds 3.51
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 3.51$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FC723669-28F5-4DD7-8D03-9D1BA3DA811D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Exercise books come in packs of $25$. The school needs $480$ exercise books.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many packs must be bought?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Number packs needed}     &= \dfrac {480} {25} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {5 \times 96} {5 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 96} {\cancel {5} \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {96} {5} \\\\
                                &= 19 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 20
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Number packs needed}     &= \dfrac {480} {25} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {5 \times 96} {5 \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 96} {\cancel {5} \times 5} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {96} {5} \\\\
                                &= 19 \ \text {remainder} \ 1 \\\\
                                &\approx 20
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Each pack costs $\pounds6$.

Work out the total cost of the exercise books.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the answer above,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 20 \times 6 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 120
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 120$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the answer above,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 20 \times 6 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 120
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 120$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \ \text {of} \ \pounds 25.74 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {3} \times 25.74 \\\\
&= \dfrac {25.74} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 8.58} {3} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times 8.58} {\cancel {3}} \\\\
&= \pounds 8.58
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

$\pounds 8.58$

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$\dfrac{3} {4}$ of $180\degree$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 180 \degree \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 180 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 180} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 45} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {4} \times 45} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 45 \\\\
&= 135 \degree
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$135 \degree$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \ \text {of} \ 180 \degree \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {4} \times 180 \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 180} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4 \times 45} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {4} \times 45} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 45 \\\\
&= 135 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7ED0E8A6-8797-4138-A006-85F2D567D9C5'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Mrs Cook’s supermarket basket contains: 

$1$ loaf of bread costing $68\text{p}$

$1$ packet of cheese costing $\pounds3.29$ 

$5$ packets of crisps at $22\text{p}$ each 

$2$ tins of soup at $39\text{p}$ each.

Calculate how much change she will receive if she pays with a $\pounds20$ note. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 68 \text {p} + \pounds 3.29 + 5 \times 22 \text {p} + 2 \times 39 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.68 + \pounds 3.29 + 5 \times \pounds 0.22 + 2 \times \pounds 0.39 \\\\
                        &= 3.97 + 1.1 + 0.78 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 5.85 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 14.15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 14.15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 68 \text {p} + \pounds 3.29 + 5 \times 22 \text {p} + 2 \times 39 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 0.68 + \pounds 3.29 + 5 \times \pounds 0.22 + 2 \times \pounds 0.39 \\\\
                        &= 3.97 + 1.1 + 0.78 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.85 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 5.85 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 14.15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 14.15$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$30\%$ of $\pounds 70$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \text { of } \pounds 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 70} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \cancel {0} \times 7 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 7 \\\\
&= \pounds 21
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 21$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 30 \% \text { of } \pounds 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30} {100} \times 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {30 \times 70} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \cancel {0} \times 7 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 7 \\\\
&= \pounds 21
\end{aligned}

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

a fifth of $\pounds 70$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {5} \times 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {70} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 14} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 14} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= \pounds 14
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 14$

&= \dfrac {5 \times 14} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 14} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= \pounds 14
\end{aligned}

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$80\%$ of a half of $\pounds 70$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {80} {100} \times \dfrac {1} {2} \times 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {80 \times 70} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 40 \times 70} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 4 \cancel {0} \times 7 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 7 \\\\
&= \pounds 28
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 28$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {80} {100} \times \dfrac {1} {2} \times 70 \\\\
&= \dfrac {80 \times 70} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 40 \times 70} {100 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 4 \cancel {0} \times 7 \cancel {0}} {1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 7 \\\\
&= \pounds 28
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

<div class='question question'>

Mark has a market stall.
He sells apples at $56 \text{p}$ for each kilogram.

She pays for her apples with a $\pounds 5$ note.

</div>
<div class='workings'>

<div class='question subquestion'>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 56 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= 224 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.24 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 2.24 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.76
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 2.76$

</div>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 56 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= 224 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.24 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 2.24 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.76
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Mark bought $25$ melons for his stall. 
He paid $\pounds 16$ for 25 melons.

Work out the price Mark paid for each melon, in pence.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost of each melon}  &= \dfrac {\pounds 16} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {1600 \text {p}} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 64} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 64} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
                            &= 64 \text {p}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$64 \text {p}$

</div>
</div>

                            &= \dfrac {1600 \text {p}} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {25 \times 64} {25} \\\\
                            &= \dfrac {\cancel {25} \times 64} {\cancel {25}} \\\\
                            &= 64 \text {p}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Susan’s bus fare is exactly $44 \text{p}$. If the driver can give her change, what is the smallest number of coins that can change hands when Susan pays to get on the bus ? (**Explain clearly**)

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

\text{change = 56 p , So 10*5 and 5*5 and 1*1}

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$56 p , So 10*5 and 5*5 and 1*1$

</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A sandwich costs $\pounds 2.46$. A drink costs $\pounds 1.29$.

What is the combined cost?

</div>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total cost}    &= 2.46 + 1.29 \\\\
                     &= \pounds 3.75
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 3.75$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total cost}    &= 2.46 + 1.29 \\\\
                     &= \pounds 3.75
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>

</code></pre>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A box of chocolates contains $37$ individual chocolates.

How many chocolates are there, in total, in $18$ of the same boxes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Total chocolates} &= 18 \times 37\\\\
                        &= 666
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$666$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}

<div class='question question'>

Find four ninths of $963\text{p}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {9} \ \text{of} \ 963 \text{p} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {9} \times 963 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 963} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 9 \times 107} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{9} \times 107} {\cancel{9}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 107 \\\\
&= 428 \text {p}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$428 \text {p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {4} {9} \ \text{of} \ 963 \text{p} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4} {9} \times 963 \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 963} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times 9 \times 107} {9} \\\\
&= \dfrac {4 \times \cancel{9} \times 107} {\cancel{9}} \\\\
&= 4 \times 107 \\\\
&= 428 \text {p}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-2B5D56B6-9D9F-41E6-8D6E-2058BC838997'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find five eighths of $408 \text{p}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {5} {8} \ \text {of} \ 408 \text {p} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {8} \times 408 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 408} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 8 \times 51} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {8} \times 51} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 5 \times 51 \\\\
&= 255 \text {p}
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$255 \text{p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= \dfrac {5} {8} \ \text {of} \ 408 \text {p} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {8} \times 408 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 408} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 8 \times 51} {8} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {8} \times 51} {\cancel {8}} \\\\
&= 5 \times 51 \\\\
&= 255 \text {p}
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A44E4F3A-9D7D-4F20-A66B-292B5CDE97B1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Sam has $\pounds 3.85$ to spend. He buys a pencil for $38 \text{p}$ and a ruler for $59 \text{p}$. How much does he have left?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required


$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent} &= 38  + 59  \\\\
                    &= 97 \text{p} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 0.97 \\\\
\text{Money left}   &= 3.85 - 0.97 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 2.88
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 2.88$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent} &= 38  + 59  \\\\
                    &= 97 \text{p} \\\\
                    &= \pounds 0.97 \\\\
\text{Money left}   &= 3.85 - 0.97 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 2.88
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 2.88$
</code></pre>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 250$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 250} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 25 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 25
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 250} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 25 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel {0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 25
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $5\%$ of $\pounds 250$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 250} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 25 \times 10} {2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 25 \times \cancel {10}} {2 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {2} \\\\
&= \pounds 12.50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 12.50$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 5 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {100} \times 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 250} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 25 \times 10} {2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 25 \times \cancel {10}} {2 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {2} \\\\
&= \pounds 12.50
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 12.50$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $2 1/2\%$ of $\pounds 250$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {2 \times 100} \times 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 250} {2 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 25 \times 10} {2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 25 \times \cancel {10}} {2 \times 2 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {4} \\\\
&= \pounds 6.25
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 6.25$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {2 \times 100} \times 250 \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 250} {2 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5 \times 25 \times 10} {2 \times 2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 25 \times \cancel {10}} {2 \times 2 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {2 \times 2} \\\\
&= \dfrac {25} {4} \\\\
&= \pounds 6.25
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 6.25$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Use your answers to find $17 \dfrac {1} {2}\%$ of $\pounds 250$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Using answers above,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 17 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 250 \\\\
&= (10 \% + 5 \% + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \%) \times 250 \\\\
&= 10 \% \times 250 + 5 \% \times  250 + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \times  250 \\\\
&= 25 + 12.50 + 6.25 \\\\
&= \pounds 43.75
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 43.75$

</div>
</div>

&= (10 \% + 5 \% + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \%) \times 250 \\\\
&= 10 \% \times 250 + 5 \% \times  250 + 2 \dfrac {1} {2} \% \times  250 \\\\
&= 25 + 12.50 + 6.25 \\\\
&= \pounds 43.75
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B474E464-5AEF-419A-9B3D-08581A43D0DE'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

On holiday last year Phil bought $10$ post cards at $45 \text{p}$ each and $10\ \text{second}$ class stamps at $27 \text{p}$ each. How much change did he get from a $\pounds 10$ note?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 10 \times 45 + 10 \times 27 \\\\
                            &= 450 + 270 \\\\
                            &= 720 \text {p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 7.2 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 10 - 7.2 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 2.80
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 2.80$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 10 \times 45 + 10 \times 27 \\\\
                            &= 450 + 270 \\\\
                            &= 720 \text {p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 7.2 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 10 - 7.2 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 2.80
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 2.80$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-07B7F155-62F9-4100-8C73-1796ABA98DCB'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A family with two adults and four children go to the circus. If an adult ticket costs $\pounds 11.50$ and a child ticket costs $\pounds 3.70$, how much change will they get if they pay with two $\pounds 20$ notes?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 2 \times  11.50 + 4 \times 3.70 \\\\
                        &= 23 + 14.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 37.80 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 2 \times 20 - 37.80 \\\\
                        &= 40 - 37.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 2.20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 2 \times  11.50 + 4 \times 3.70 \\\\
                        &= 23 + 14.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 37.80 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 2 \times 20 - 37.80 \\\\
                        &= 40 - 37.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 2.20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 2.20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 950$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 950 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 950 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 950} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 95 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 95
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 95$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

&= \dfrac {10 \times 950} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 95 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 95
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Julie owed Tasweer $\pounds 12.70$. She paid her back with a $\pounds 20$ note but she did not have any change. How much does Tasweer now owe Julie?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tasweer owe Julie}    &= 20 - 12.70 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 7.30
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 7.30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Tasweer owe Julie}    &= 20 - 12.70 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 7.30
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 7.30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-6A7A8296-115E-4018-A6E7-A4AA31CB5F0F'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $20\%$ of $30\%$ of $40\%$ of $\pounds 50$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 30 \% \ \text {of} \ 40 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 50 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {100} \times \dfrac {30} {100} \times \dfrac {40} {100} \times 50 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 30 \times 40 \times 50} {100 \times 100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \times 30 \times 40 \times 50} {100 \times 100 \times 2 \times 50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 1 \cancel {0} \times 3 \cancel {0} \times 4 \cancel {0} \times \cancel {50}} {10\cancel {0} \times 1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {50}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {10} \\\\
&= \pounds 1.20
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 1.20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
&= 20 \% \ \text {of} \ 30 \% \ \text {of} \ 40 \% \ \text {of} \ \pounds 50 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20} {100} \times \dfrac {30} {100} \times \dfrac {40} {100} \times 50 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 30 \times 40 \times 50} {100 \times 100 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \times 10 \times 30 \times 40 \times 50} {100 \times 100 \times 2 \times 50} \\\\
&= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 1 \cancel {0} \times 3 \cancel {0} \times 4 \cancel {0} \times \cancel {50}} {10\cancel {0} \times 1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {50}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 4} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {12} {10} \\\\
&= \pounds 1.20
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 1.20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-56C543D5-BB5B-4474-8B73-44CAEEF83470'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A family with $\text{two}$ adults and $\text{three}$ children go to the Zoo. If an adult ticket costs $\pounds 9.50$ and a child ticket costs $\pounds 2.60$, how much change will they get if they pay with a $\pounds 50$ note? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 2 \times 9.50 + 3 \times 2.60 \\\\
                        &= 19 + 7.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 26.80 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 50 - 26.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 23.20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 23.20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}      &= 2 \times 9.50 + 3 \times 2.60 \\\\
                        &= 19 + 7.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 26.80 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 50 - 26.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 23.20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 23.20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $10\%$ of $\pounds 760$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 760 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 760 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 760} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 76 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 76
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 76$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 10 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 760 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10} {100} \times 760 \\\\
&= \dfrac {10 \times 760} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {1 \cancel{0} \times 76 \cancel{0}} {1 \cancel{0} \cancel{0}} \\\\
&= \pounds 76
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is $15\%$ of $\pounds 760$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 15 \% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 760 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15} {100} \times 760 \\\\
&= \dfrac {15 \times 760} {100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 5 \times 2 \times 38 \times 10} {2 \times 5 \times 10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times 38 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 38 \\\\
&= \pounds 114
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 114$

</div>
</div>

&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {2} \times 38 \times \cancel {10}} {\cancel {2} \times \cancel {5} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 38 \\\\
&= \pounds 114
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-EFD43A9D-C622-4E56-8393-2CA2B39FA64E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Tina owed Josh $\pounds 23.80$. She paid him back with three $\pounds 10$ notes but he did not have any change. How much does Josh now owe Tina?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Josh owe Tina}    &= 3 \times 10 - 23.80 \\\\
                        &= 30 - 23.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.20
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 6.20$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Josh owe Tina}    &= 3 \times 10 - 23.80 \\\\
                        &= 30 - 23.80 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.20
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 6.20$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7CF52F4B-090D-493A-8346-80188BAA6634'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Karen and Fran have some money. Karen has $\pounds 9$ more than Fran, together they have $\pounds 45$. How much money does Karen have?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let Fran has (in $\pounds$) $= a$

Karen has (in $\pounds$) $= a + 9$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 9           &= 45 \\\\
2a + 9              &= 45 \\\\
2a                  &= 45 - 9 \\\\
2a                  &= 36 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {36} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 18} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 18} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                   &= 18 \\\\
\text{Karen has}    &= a + 9 \\\\
                    &= 18 + 9 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let Fran has (in $\pounds$) $= a$

Karen has (in $\pounds$) $= a + 9$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + a + 9           &= 45 \\\\
2a + 9              &= 45 \\\\
2a                  &= 45 - 9 \\\\
2a                  &= 36 \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {36} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {2 \times 18} {2} \\\\
a                   &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 18} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                   &= 18 \\\\
\text{Karen has}    &= a + 9 \\\\
                    &= 18 + 9 \\\\
                    &= \pounds 27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 27$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E939A9F2-9AF4-43F6-9C5F-9F1C96A47959'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

What is $\dfrac{1}{4}$ of $20\%$ of $\dfrac{1}{6}$ of $40\%$ of $\pounds 1200$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text{of} \ 20\% \ \text{of} \ \dfrac {1} {6} \ \text{of} \ 40\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times \dfrac {20} {100} \times \dfrac {1} {6} \times \dfrac {40} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 40 \times 1200} {4 \times 100 \times 6 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 10 \times 2 \times 6 \times 100} {4 \times 100 \times 6 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times \cancel {4} \times 1 \cancel {0} \times 2 \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel {4} \times 1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 2 \\\\
&= \pounds 4
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 4$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \ \text{of} \ 20\% \ \text{of} \ \dfrac {1} {6} \ \text{of} \ 40\% \ \text{of} \ \pounds 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {1} {4} \times \dfrac {20} {100} \times \dfrac {1} {6} \times \dfrac {40} {100} \times 1200 \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 40 \times 1200} {4 \times 100 \times 6 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {20 \times 4 \times 10 \times 2 \times 6 \times 100} {4 \times 100 \times 6 \times 100} \\\\
&= \dfrac {2 \cancel {0} \times \cancel {4} \times 1 \cancel {0} \times 2 \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {100}} {\cancel {4} \times 1 \cancel {0} \cancel {0} \times \cancel {6} \times \cancel {100}} \\\\
&= 2 \times 2 \\\\
&= \pounds 4
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 4$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-29A6018D-4F9B-47BC-AEF7-2AB556029423'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

If an adult ticket on the bus costs $\pounds 1.50$ and a child ticket costs $90 \text{p}$, how much change will a family of $2$ adults and their $3$ children get if they pay with a $\pounds 10$ note?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times \pounds 1.50 + 3 \times 90 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3 + 3 \times \pounds 0.90 \\\\
                        &= 3 + 2.70 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.70 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 5.70 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.30
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 4.30$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 2 \times \pounds 1.50 + 3 \times 90 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3 + 3 \times \pounds 0.90 \\\\
                        &= 3 + 2.70 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 5.70 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 10 - 5.70 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 4.30
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 4.30$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='workings'>

.

$

                                                                            &= \pounds 4.40

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How many cups of tea can be bought with $\pounds5$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cups of tea}     &= \dfrac {\pounds 5} {85 \text {p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {500 \text {p}} {85 \text {p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {500} {85} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 100} {5 \times 17} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 100} {\cancel {5} \times 17} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {100} {17} \\\\
                        &= 5 \ \text {remainder} \ 15 \\\\
                        &\approx 5
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>

                        &= \dfrac {500 \text {p}} {85 \text {p}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {500} {85} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 100} {5 \times 17} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {5} \times 100} {\cancel {5} \times 17} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {100} {17} \\\\
                        &= 5 \ \text {remainder} \ 15 \\\\
                        &\approx 5
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-0E978EF0-5AA5-41D7-BA1E-A165B13DC328'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Maya made three purchases for $\pounds 1.08, \pounds 5.04$ and $\pounds 9.89$. What did she spend in total, to the nearest pound?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}   &= 1.08 +  5.04 + 9.89 \\\\
                      &= 16.01 \\\\
                      &  \approx \pounds 16
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 16$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}   &= 1.08 +  5.04 + 9.89 \\\\
                      &= 16.01 \\\\
                      &  \approx \pounds 16
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 16$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FEDF1274-F3D1-48A8-9364-946A301048E2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Six people pay $\pounds 5.50$ each for a meal in a restaurant. They leave a $10 \%$ tip for the waiter. How much change do they receive from a $\pounds 50$ note?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

Total expenditure  $= 6 \times \pounds5.50$

$\pounds 33$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Change}                   &=  \text{Total money} -  \text{Total expenditure} - \text{Tip} \\\\
                                &=  50 -  33 - \dfrac{1\cancel {0} }{1\cancel {0} \cancel {0} } \times 5 \cancel {0} \\\\
                                &=  50 -  33 - 5 \\\\
                                &=  50 -  28 \\\\
                                &=  22
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,

Total expenditure  $= 6 \times \pounds5.50$

$\pounds 33$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Change}                   &=  \text{Total money} -  \text{Total expenditure} - \text{Tip} \\\\
                                &=  50 -  33 - \dfrac{1\cancel {0} }{1\cancel {0} \cancel {0} } \times 5 \cancel {0} \\\\
                                &=  50 -  33 - 5 \\\\
                                &=  50 -  28 \\\\
                                &=  22
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$22$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>


</div>
</li>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-81AADA28-3EE1-4D17-82EE-CBBF6BF4E0D7'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Eliot made three purchases for $\pounds 3.12, \pounds 10.25$ and $\pounds 4.80$. 

What did he spend in total, to the nearest pound?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}  &= 3.12 + 10.25 + 4.80 \\\\
                     &= 18.17 \\\\
                     & \approx \pounds 18
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 18$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}  &= 3.12 + 10.25 + 4.80 \\\\
                     &= 18.17 \\\\
                     & \approx \pounds 18
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 18$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B6EEAA19-D6EB-4A7E-8078-F1B4EA5EB197'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

If I buy $6$ pens at $\pounds 1.15$ each and 10 refills at $56 \text{p}$ each, how much change will I get if I pay with a $\pounds 20$ note?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 6 \times \pounds 1.15 + 10 \times 56 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.90 + 10 \times \pounds 0.56 \\\\
                        &= 6.90 + 5.60 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 12.50 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 12.50 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 7.50
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 7.50$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 6 \times \pounds 1.15 + 10 \times 56 \text {p} \\\\
                        &= \pounds 6.90 + 10 \times \pounds 0.56 \\\\
                        &= 6.90 + 5.60 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 12.50 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 20 - 12.50 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 7.50
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 7.50$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B14230C1-397F-4229-962F-877D59B5C1B2'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Danielle went to the store to buy stationery for school. She bought four rulers, five pens and three pencils. The rulers cost $46 \text{p}$ each, the pens cost $37 \text{p}$ each and the pencils cost $13 \text{p}$ each.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How much did she pay in total for her stationery?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 4 \times 46 + 5 \times 37 + 3 \times 3 \\\\
                    &= 184 + 185 + 9 \\\\
                    &= 378 \text {p}  \\\\
                    &= \pounds 3.78
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

\pounds 3.78

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total cost}  &= 4 \times 46 + 5 \times 37 + 3 \times 3 \\\\
                    &= 184 + 185 + 9 \\\\
                    &= 378 \text {p}  \\\\
                    &= \pounds 3.78
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>\pounds 3.78
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

If she paid with a $\pounds 10$ note, how much change did she get?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Change received}     &= \pounds 10 - \pounds 3.78 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 6.22
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 6.22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Change received}     &= \pounds 10 - \pounds 3.78 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 6.22
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 6.22$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-26BE9BBB-6A7B-493E-92F7-34048794B52B'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

If you buy $8$ second class stamps costing $44 \text{p}$ each, how much change would you receive from $\pounds 5$? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Change received}     &= \pounds 5 - 8 \times 44 \text {p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5 - 8 \times \pounds 0.44 \\\\
                            &= 5 - 3.52 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 1.48
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 1.48$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Change received}     &= \pounds 5 - 8 \times 44 \text {p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 5 - 8 \times \pounds 0.44 \\\\
                            &= 5 - 3.52 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 1.48
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 1.48$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-88771F84-2908-43E4-9380-A12099F3BB56'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Ian buys

- $3$ bananas at $12 \text{p}$ per banana
- $5$ apples at $11 \text{p}$ per apple

If Ian pays with a $\pounds 2$ coin how much change would Ian get? Give your answer in pounds and pence. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 3 \times 12  + 5 \times 11 \\\\
                            &= 36 + 55 \\\\
                            &= 91 \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.91 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 2 - 0.91 \\\\
                            &= 1.09 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 1 \ \text {and} \ 9 \text {p}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 1 \ \text {and} \ 9 \text {p}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}         &= 3 \times 12  + 5 \times 11 \\\\
                            &= 36 + 55 \\\\
                            &= 91 \text{p} \\\\
                            &= \pounds 0.91 \\\\
\text {Change received}     &= 2 - 0.91 \\\\
                            &= 1.09 \\\\
                            &= \pounds 1 \ \text {and} \ 9 \text {p}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 1 \ \text {and} \ 9 \text {p}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-F7CB4C96-99C6-4696-913C-ACB1B87877A1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Imogen buys

 4 star stickers at 15p per sticker

 7 moon stickers at 16p per sticker

If Imogen pays with a £5 note how much change would Imogen get? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: It is really important to use the right units in calculations and convert where required
.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 15 + 7 \times 16 \\\\
                        &= 60 + 112 \\\\
                        &= 172 \text {p}  \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.72 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 1.72 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.28
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 3.28$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_unit_no_dot_note.

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Money spent}     &= 4 \times 15 + 7 \times 16 \\\\
                        &= 60 + 112 \\\\
                        &= 172 \text {p}  \\\\
                        &= \pounds 1.72 \\\\
\text {Change received} &= 5 - 1.72 \\\\
                        &= \pounds 3.28
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 3.28$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-4D17A6EE-BFCA-43FF-BD1A-329ADAFFC202'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

I am counting up the money from the tombola at the end of the school fair.  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

There are twenty four $\pounds 1$ coins, seven $\pounds 2$ coins , six $\pounds 5$ notes,fourteen $\pounds 10$ notes, three $\pounds 20$ notes and one $\pounds 50$ note.

How much money is there in total?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)
 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money}    &= 24 \times 1 + 7 \times 2 + 6 \times 5 + 14 \times 10 + 3 \times 20 + 50 \\\\
                       &= 24 + 14 + 30 + 140 + 60 + 50 \\\\
                       &= \pounds 318
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 318$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total money}    &= 24 \times 1 + 7 \times 2 + 6 \times 5 + 14 \times 10 + 3 \times 20 + 50 \\\\
                       &= 24 + 14 + 30 + 140 + 60 + 50 \\\\
                       &= \pounds 318
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 318$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

I exchange all of the money for as many $\pounds 20$ notes as possible.
How many $\pounds 20$ notes do I have now?

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Count of } \pounds 20 \text{ notes}    &= \dfrac {318} {20} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 159} {2 \times 10} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 159} {\cancel {2} \times 10} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {159} {10} \\\\
                                                &= 15 \text{ remainder } 9 \\\\
                                                & \approx 15
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Count of } \pounds 20 \text{ notes}    &= \dfrac {318} {20} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {2 \times 159} {2 \times 10} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 159} {\cancel {2} \times 10} \\\\
                                                &= \dfrac {159} {10} \\\\
                                                &= 15 \text{ remainder } 9 \\\\
                                                & \approx 15
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-655C3EF2-4B38-45E9-8354-01DF35EC3875'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The museum took a total of $\pounds 600$ in entrance fees one Friday. How many people paid to visit the museum that Friday? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total people}    &= \dfrac {600} {1.20} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {600 \times 100} {1.20 \times 100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {600 \times 100} {120} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 10 \times 100} {12 \times 10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {12} \times \cancel {10} \times 100} {\cancel {12} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                        &= 5 \times 100 \\\\
                        &= 500
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$500$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Total people}    &= \dfrac {600} {1.20} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {600 \times 100} {1.20 \times 100} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {600 \times 100} {120} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 12 \times 10 \times 100} {12 \times 10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel {12} \times \cancel {10} \times 100} {\cancel {12} \times \cancel {10}} \\\\
                        &= 5 \times 100 \\\\
                        &= 500
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A1426515-EEB1-42FF-91BB-F3BFD31D7485'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>


Tickets for the cinema cost $\pounds 6.30$ for adults and $\pounds 3.40$ for children.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How much would it cost for $6$ adults to go to the cinema?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost for} \ 6 \ \text {adults}    &= 6 \times 6.30 \\\\
                                         &= \pounds 37.80
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 37.80$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost for} \ 6 \ \text {adults}    &= 6 \times 6.30 \\\\
                                         &= \pounds 37.80
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 37.80$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

How much would it cost for 3 adults and 4 children?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost for} \ 3 \ \text {adults and} \ 4 \ \text {children}    &= 3 \times 6.30 + 4 \times 3.40 \\\\
                                                                    &= 18.90 + 13.60 \\\\
                                                                    &= \pounds 32.50
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 32.50$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Cost for} \ 3 \ \text {adults and} \ 4 \ \text {children}    &= 3 \times 6.30 + 4 \times 3.40 \\\\
                                                                    &= 18.90 + 13.60 \\\\
                                                                    &= \pounds 32.50
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 32.50$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the maximum number of people that can go to the
cinema for $\pounds 20$?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Number of children}      &= \dfrac {20} {3.40} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 100} {3.40 \times 100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 100} {340} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 100} {20 \times 17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times 100} {\cancel {20} \times 17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {100} {17} \\\\
                                &= 5 \ \text {remainder} \ 15 \\\\
                                &\approx 5 \\\\
\text {Number of adults}        &= \dfrac {20 - 5 \times 3.40} {6.30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 - 17} {6.30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3} {6.30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {6.30 \times 100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {630} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 10 \times 10} {3 \times 10 \times 21} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {10} \times 10} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {10} \times 21} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {10} {21} \\\\
                                &= 0 \ \text {remainder} \ 10 \\\\
                                &\approx 0 \\\\
\text {Total number of people}  &= 5 + 0 \\\\
                                &= 5
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin {aligned}
\text {Number of children}      &= \dfrac {20} {3.40} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 100} {3.40 \times 100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 100} {340} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 \times 100} {20 \times 17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {20} \times 100} {\cancel {20} \times 17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {100} {17} \\\\
                                &= 5 \ \text {remainder} \ 15 \\\\
                                &\approx 5 \\\\
\text {Number of adults}        &= \dfrac {20 - 5 \times 3.40} {6.30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {20 - 17} {6.30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3} {6.30} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {6.30 \times 100} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 100} {630} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {3 \times 10 \times 10} {3 \times 10 \times 21} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {10} \times 10} {\cancel {3} \times \cancel {10} \times 21} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {10} {21} \\\\
                                &= 0 \ \text {remainder} \ 10 \\\\
                                &\approx 0 \\\\
\text {Total number of people}  &= 5 + 0 \\\\
                                &= 5
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>
